| `toriyose::danpen::block_end(x, j, line, col)` |  |  |  | block_end finds the index of the triple quote that closes a block string, skipping an escaped one. |
| `toriyose::danpen::block_at(x, i, line, col)` |  |  |  | block_at reads a block string token from `i`, counting the lines it spans so later columns stay right. |
| `toriyose::danpen::indent_of(line)` |  |  |  | indent_of counts the spaces and tabs that lead a line. |
| `toriyose::danpen::least(xs)` |  |  |  | least is the smallest of a non-empty list of numbers. |
| `toriyose::danpen::blank?(line)` |  |  |  | blank? is true for a line of only spaces and tabs. |
| `toriyose::danpen::block_value(raw)` |  |  |  | block_value is a block string's value: the common indent of its later lines removed and its leading and trailing blank lines dropped. |
| `toriyose::danpen::trim_blank(lines)` |  |  |  | trim_blank drops the blank lines at both ends of a list of lines. |
| `toriyose::danpen::drop_blank(lines)` |  |  |  | drop_blank drops the blank lines at the start of a list of lines. |
| `toriyose::danpen::eof` |  |  |  | eof is the token the parser sees past the last one, so an error there can say "end of input". |
| `toriyose::danpen::tok(p, i)` |  |  |  | tok is the token at index `i`, or an end-of-input token placed just after the last one. |
| `toriyose::danpen::shown(t)` |  |  |  | shown is how a parse error names the token it found. |
| `toriyose::danpen::fail(p, i, wanted)` |  |  |  | fail raises a parse error at the token at `i`, saying what was wanted and what was found. |
| `toriyose::danpen::tok_is?(p, i, kind, v)` |  |  |  | tok_is?: True when the token at `i` is of `kind` and reads `v`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::consume(p, i, kind, v)` |  |  |  | consume: The index after the token at `i` when it is of `kind` and reads `v`; otherwise a parse error naming `v`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::punct?(p, i, v)` |  |  |  | punct? is true when the token at `i` is the punctuator `v`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::word?(p, i, v)` |  |  |  | word? is true when the token at `i` is the name `v`, as a keyword is read. |
| `toriyose::danpen::expect(p, i, v)` |  |  |  | expect steps past the punctuator `v` at `i`, or raises a parse error naming it. |
| `toriyose::danpen::keyword(p, i, v)` |  |  |  | keyword steps past the keyword `v` at `i`, or raises a parse error naming it. |
| `toriyose::danpen::name_at(p, i)` |  |  |  | name_at reads the name at `i`, or raises a parse error that wanted one. |
| `toriyose::danpen::mark(p, i, node)` |  |  |  | mark stamps a node with the line and column of the token at `i` when the parse asked for spans, and leaves it alone otherwise. |
| `toriyose::danpen::many(p, i, item, close, acc)` |  |  |  | many reads items with `item` until the punctuator `close`, answering the items and the index after `close`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::one_or_more(p, i, open, item, close)` |  |  |  | one_or_more reads a bracketed list that must hold at least one item, as GraphQL requires of every bracket it opens. |
| `toriyose::danpen::optional_list(p, i, open, item, close)` |  |  |  | optional_list reads a bracketed list when one opens at `i`, and answers an empty list without moving otherwise. |
| `toriyose::danpen::document_of(p, i, acc)` |  |  |  | document_of reads every definition from `i` to the end into a document, refusing an empty one. |
| `toriyose::danpen::definition(p, i)` |  |  |  | definition reads the one definition at `i`, choosing operation, fragment, extension or type system by its first token. |
| `toriyose::danpen::operation(p, i)` |  |  |  | operation reads an operation at `i`, a bare selection set being an anonymous query. |
| `toriyose::danpen::keyword_of(v)` |  |  |  | keyword_of turns an operation keyword into its operation type, nil for any other word. |
| `toriyose::danpen::variable_definition(p, i)` |  |  |  | variable_definition reads one `$name: Type = default` of an operation's variable list. |
| `toriyose::danpen::default_value(p, i)` |  |  |  | default_value reads the constant after `=` when one is there, and answers nil without moving otherwise. |
| `toriyose::danpen::type_ref(p, i)` |  |  |  | type_ref reads a type reference: a name, a list of a type, either made non-null by `!`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::directives(p, i, constant)` |  |  |  | directives reads the directives at `i`, none being an empty list; `constant` refuses variables in their arguments. |
| `toriyose::danpen::directives_from(p, i, constant, acc)` |  |  |  | directives_from is the loop of `directives`, adding each directive read to `acc`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::arguments(p, i, constant)` |  |  |  | arguments reads a parenthesized argument list when one opens at `i`, and an empty list otherwise. |
| `toriyose::danpen::argument(p, i, constant)` |  |  |  | argument reads one `name: value` argument. |
| `toriyose::danpen::value(p, i, constant)` |  |  |  | value reads one input value at `i`; when `constant` is true a variable is a parse error, as in a default or a schema directive. |
| `toriyose::danpen::object_field(p, i, constant)` |  |  |  | object_field reads one `name: value` field of an input object value. |
| `toriyose::danpen::selection_set(p, i)` |  |  |  | selection_set reads a braced, non-empty list of selections. |
| `toriyose::danpen::selection(p, i)` |  |  |  | selection reads one selection at `i`: a field, a fragment spread or an inline fragment. |
| `toriyose::danpen::spread(p, i)` |  |  |  | spread reads a named fragment spread `...Name` and its directives. |
| `toriyose::danpen::inline_fragment(p, i)` |  |  |  | inline_fragment reads `... on Type { ... }`, the type condition being optional. |
| `toriyose::danpen::field(p, i)` |  |  |  | field reads one field selection: its alias, name, arguments, directives and sub-selections. |
| `toriyose::danpen::fragment_definition(p, i)` |  |  |  | fragment_definition reads `fragment Name on Type { ... }`, refusing a fragment named `on`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::type_system(p, i, description)` |  |  |  | type_system reads one type system definition at `i` and attaches the description that came before it. |
| `toriyose::danpen::extension(p, i)` |  |  |  | extension reads the definition after `extend`, refusing one that adds nothing. |
| `toriyose::danpen::extends_something?(node)` |  |  |  | extends_something? is true when an extension node adds at least one directive, operation, interface, field, member or value. |
| `toriyose::danpen::type_body(p, i, v)` |  |  |  | type_body reads the body of a type system definition whose keyword `v` sits at `i`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::schema_definition(p, i)` |  |  |  | schema_definition reads `schema { query: Q ... }`, the root operation types a schema names. |
| `toriyose::danpen::root_operation(p, i)` |  |  |  | root_operation reads one `query: Type` entry of a schema definition. |
| `toriyose::danpen::object_like(p, i, v)` |  |  |  | object_like reads an object type or an interface, which share one shape: interfaces, directives and fields. |
| `toriyose::danpen::separated_names(p, i, sep)` |  |  |  | separated_names reads names joined by `sep`, a leading `sep` allowed, as in `implements & A & B`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::more_names(p, i, sep, acc)` |  |  |  | more_names is the loop of `separated_names`, adding each name after a `sep` to `acc`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::field_definition(p, i)` |  |  |  | field_definition reads one field of an object or interface type, with its description and arguments. |
| `toriyose::danpen::description_at(p, i)` |  |  |  | description_at reads the description string at `i` when there is one, and nil without moving otherwise. |
| `toriyose::danpen::input_value(p, i)` |  |  |  | input_value reads one argument definition or input field: description, name, type, default and directives. |
| `toriyose::danpen::union_definition(p, i)` |  |  |  | union_definition reads `union U = A \| B`, its members optional so an extension can add directives alone. |
| `toriyose::danpen::enum_definition(p, i)` |  |  |  | enum_definition reads `enum E { A B }` and its values. |
| `toriyose::danpen::enum_value(p, i)` |  |  |  | enum_value reads one enum value, refusing `true`, `false` and `null` as its name. |
| `toriyose::danpen::directive_definition(p, i)` |  |  |  | directive_definition reads `directive @name(...) repeatable on LOCATION \| ...`, refusing a location GraphQL does not define. |
| `toriyose::danpen::pad(ind)` |  |  |  | pad is the indentation of nesting depth `ind`, two spaces a level. |
| `toriyose::danpen::deeper(ind)` |  |  |  | deeper is the next nesting depth, staying nil for the compact one-line layout. |
| `toriyose::danpen::nl(ind, space)` |  |  |  | nl is the break between items: a newline and indent at depth `ind`, or in the compact layout a space when `space` asks for one. |
| `toriyose::danpen::commas(ind, open, close, items, f)` |  |  |  | commas writes items between `open` and `close`, comma separated, one per line with a trailing comma when indented. |
| `toriyose::danpen::curly(ind, items, f)` |  |  |  | curly writes items as a braced block, one per line when indented and space separated when compact. |
| `toriyose::danpen::control_chars` |  |  |  | control_chars pairs each control character a quoted string must escape, tab aside, with its code. |
| `toriyose::danpen::quoted(text)` |  |  |  | quoted writes text as a one-line GraphQL string, escaping quotes, backslashes and control characters. |
| `toriyose::danpen::byte_length(text)` |  |  |  | byte_length counts the UTF-8 bytes of text, the measure apollo-compiler uses to break a long block string. |
| `toriyose::danpen::block_able?(text)` |  |  |  | block_able? is true when text survives a round trip as a block string: no carriage return, no blank first or last line, no common indent. |
| `toriyose::danpen::block(ind, text)` |  |  |  | block writes text as a block string, on its own lines at depth `ind` when it is long or spans lines. |
| `toriyose::danpen::string_text(ind, described, text)` |  |  |  | string_text picks the block or quoted form for a string the way apollo-compiler does: block for a description or a multi-line value that allows it. |
| `toriyose::danpen::description_text(ind, d)` |  |  |  | description_text writes a description followed by its break, or nothing when there is none. |
| `toriyose::danpen::type_text(t)` |  |  |  | type_text writes a type reference back as GraphQL. |
| `toriyose::danpen::value_text(ind, v)` |  |  |  | value_text writes an input value back as GraphQL at depth `ind`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::arguments_text(args)` |  |  |  | arguments_text writes a field's or directive's arguments on one line, nothing when there are none. |
| `toriyose::danpen::directives_text(dirs)` |  |  |  | directives_text writes directives, each after a space, to follow the thing they annotate. |
| `toriyose::danpen::default_text(ind, dv)` |  |  |  | default_text writes ` = value` for a default, nothing when there is none. |
| `toriyose::danpen::variables_text(vars)` |  |  |  | variables_text writes an operation's variable definitions on one line, nothing when there are none. |
| `toriyose::danpen::input_value_text(ind, x)` |  |  |  | input_value_text writes one argument definition or input field with its description, default and directives. |
| `toriyose::danpen::arguments_definition_text(ind, args)` |  |  |  | arguments_definition_text writes a field's argument definitions, one per line only when one carries a description or directive. |
| `toriyose::danpen::field_definition_text(ind, f)` |  |  |  | field_definition_text writes one field of an object or interface type. |
| `toriyose::danpen::enum_value_text(ind, v)` |  |  |  | enum_value_text writes one enum value with its description and directives. |
| `toriyose::danpen::selection_text(ind, sel)` |  |  |  | selection_text writes one field, spread or inline fragment with everything nested under it. |
| `toriyose::danpen::selections_suffix(ind, sels)` |  |  |  | selections_suffix writes a field's sub-selections after a space, nothing for a leaf field. |
| `toriyose::danpen::block_suffix(ind, items, f)` |  |  |  | block_suffix writes a definition's braced members with `f` after a space, nothing when there are none. |
| `toriyose::danpen::extend_prefix(ind, d)` |  |  |  | extend_prefix starts a type system definition: `extend ` for an extension, its description otherwise. |
| `toriyose::danpen::definition_text(ind, d, leading)` |  |  |  | definition_text writes one definition; a plain query writes as its bare selection set only when it leads the document. |
| `toriyose::danpen::document_text(ind, doc)` |  |  |  | document_text writes every definition of a document, a blank line apart, or on one line when compact. |
| `toriyose::danpen::parse(text, opts?)` |  |  |  | parse: GraphQL SDL or operation text to a document tree. |
| `toriyose::danpen::render(document, format?, opts?)` |  |  |  | render: a document tree to canonical GraphQL text. |
| `toriyose::danpen::builtin_sdl` |  |  |  | builtin_sdl is the SDL every schema holds without writing it: the five scalars, the four builtin directives and the introspection types. |
| `toriyose::danpen::finding(rule, message, node)` |  |  |  | finding is one validation result: the rule broken, a message, and the node's position when the parse kept spans. |
| `toriyose::danpen::type_kinds` |  |  |  | type_kinds lists the kinds of named type a schema can define. |
| `toriyose::danpen::definitions_of(schema)` |  |  |  | definitions_of is a document's definitions, none for a missing schema. |
| `toriyose::danpen::merged(base, ext)` |  |  |  | merged folds an extension into its base definition, appending each member list the extension carries. |
| `toriyose::danpen::add_definition(state, d)` |  |  |  | add_definition indexes one type, directive or schema definition, noting a finding when its name is already taken. |
| `toriyose::danpen::add_extension(state, d)` |  |  |  | add_extension merges an extension into the type it extends, noting a finding when that type is missing or of another kind. |
| `toriyose::danpen::note(state, f)` |  |  |  | note records a finding in the indexing state. |
| `toriyose::danpen::builtins` |  |  |  | builtins are the builtin definitions, each flagged builtin so a user may redefine a builtin directive. |
| `toriyose::danpen::implicit_roots(types)` |  |  |  | implicit_roots finds the root operation types by their default names when a schema declares no `schema` block. |
| `toriyose::danpen::schema_index(schema)` |  |  |  | schema_index indexes a schema document's types, directives and roots, with the findings met while indexing. |
| `toriyose::danpen::type_named(s, name)` |  |  |  | type_named is the definition of the type `name` in an indexed schema, nil when it has none. |
| `toriyose::danpen::base_name(t)` |  |  |  | base_name is the named type under any list and non-null wrapping. |
| `toriyose::danpen::kind_in(types, name)` |  |  |  | kind_in: The kind of the type `name` in a table of types by name, nil when the table has no such type. |
| `toriyose::danpen::kind_of(s, name)` |  |  |  | kind_of is the kind of the type `name` in an indexed schema, nil when it has none. |
| `toriyose::danpen::input_kind?(k)` |  |  |  | input_kind? is true for the kinds an argument, input field or variable may have. |
| `toriyose::danpen::output_kind?(k)` |  |  |  | output_kind? is true for the kinds a field may have. |
| `toriyose::danpen::composite?(k)` |  |  |  | composite? is true for the kinds that have fields to select. |
| `toriyose::danpen::by_name(xs, name)` |  |  |  | by_name finds the item named `name` in a list of named things, nil when none is. |
| `toriyose::danpen::duplicates(names)` |  |  |  | duplicates lists, once each, the names that occur more than once. |
| `toriyose::danpen::schema_problems(s)` |  |  |  | schema_problems gathers every finding of an indexed schema: indexing, each definition and the roots. |
| `toriyose::danpen::root_problems(s)` |  |  |  | root_problems finds each root operation type that is not an object type. |
| `toriyose::danpen::known_type_problems(s, t, node, what)` |  |  |  | known_type_problems finds a type reference whose named type the schema does not define. |
| `toriyose::danpen::location_of(d)` |  |  |  | location_of names the directive location a type system definition sits at. |
| `toriyose::danpen::definition_problems(s, d)` |  |  |  | definition_problems finds the faults of one type system definition: its directives and its members. |
| `toriyose::danpen::member_problems(s, d)` |  |  |  | member_problems finds the faults of a definition's members: fields, implemented interfaces, union members, enum values or input fields. |
| `toriyose::danpen::field_definition_problems(s, f, owner)` |  |  |  | field_definition_problems finds the faults of one field definition: an undefined or input type, its directives and its arguments. |
| `toriyose::danpen::input_value_problems(s, a, location, owner)` |  |  |  | input_value_problems finds the faults of one argument definition or input field: an undefined or output type, a default that does not fit when defaults are checked, and its directives. |
| `toriyose::danpen::int32?(text)` |  |  |  | int32? is true when an Int literal fits the 32-bit signed range GraphQL's Int allows. |
| `toriyose::danpen::value_problems(s, v, t, node, where, vars)` |  |  |  | value_problems finds where an input value does not fit the type it is given for; a variable is judged elsewhere, by its position. |
| `toriyose::danpen::named_value_problems(s, v, name, node, where, vars)` |  |  |  | named_value_problems judges a value against one named type: the builtin scalars by their literal kinds, an enum by its values, an input object by its fields. |
| `toriyose::danpen::render_value(v)` |  |  |  | render_value writes a value on one line, as a finding quotes it. |
| `toriyose::danpen::object_problems(s, v, d, node, where, vars)` |  |  |  | object_problems judges an input object value against its input type: unknown, missing required and repeated fields. |
| `toriyose::danpen::argument_problems(s, args, defs, node, where, vars)` |  |  |  | argument_problems judges the arguments given against those defined: unknown, ill-typed, missing required and repeated arguments. |
| `toriyose::danpen::directive_problems(s, dirs, location, vars, _ctx)` |  |  |  | directive_problems judges directives used at one location: undefined, misplaced, wrongly argued or repeated without being repeatable. |
| `toriyose::danpen::string_type` |  |  |  | string_type is the `String!` type the meta-field `__typename` has. |
| `toriyose::danpen::field_def(s, parent, name, is_root)` |  |  |  | field_def finds the definition of field `name` on type `parent`, the introspection meta-fields included, nil when there is none. |
| `toriyose::danpen::possible_types(s, name)` |  |  |  | possible_types lists the object types a value of type `name` can be: itself, a union's members, or an interface's implementers. |
| `toriyose::danpen::overlap?(s, a, b)` |  |  |  | overlap? is true when some object type is possible for both `a` and `b`, so a fragment on one can apply inside the other. |
| `toriyose::danpen::selection_problems(ex, sels, parent, where)` |  |  |  | selection_problems gathers the findings of every selection in a set made on type `parent`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::one_selection_problems(ex, sel, parent, where)` |  |  |  | one_selection_problems judges one selection on type `parent`: a field, a spread of a known fragment that can apply, or an inline fragment on a composite type. |
| `toriyose::danpen::field_problems(ex, sel, parent, where)` |  |  |  | field_problems judges one field selection: that it exists, its arguments and directives, and that it selects fields exactly when its type has them. |
| `toriyose::danpen::spreads_in(sels)` |  |  |  | spreads_in names every fragment spread in a selection set, however deep. |
| `toriyose::danpen::reachable(frags, names, seen)` |  |  |  | reachable names every fragment the names spread, directly or through other fragments, each once. |
| `toriyose::danpen::cyclic?(frags, name)` |  |  |  | cyclic? is true when the fragment `name` spreads itself, directly or through others. |
| `toriyose::danpen::usages_in_value(v, t, default_present)` |  |  |  | usages_in_value finds each variable a value uses, with the type expected where it sits and whether that place has a default. |
| `toriyose::danpen::usages_in_arguments(args, defs)` |  |  |  | usages_in_arguments finds the variables used in arguments, typed by the argument definitions when they are known. |
| `toriyose::danpen::usages_in_directives(s, dirs)` |  |  |  | usages_in_directives finds the variables used in directive arguments, typed by the schema's directive definitions. |
| `toriyose::danpen::usages(ex, sels, parent, seen)` |  |  |  | usages finds every variable a selection set uses, following fragment spreads once each, with the type expected at each use. |
| `toriyose::danpen::compatible?(var_type, loc_type)` |  |  |  | compatible? is true when a variable of `var_type` may stand where `loc_type` is expected: non-null may narrow, never widen. |
| `toriyose::danpen::position_ok?(var_def, use)` |  |  |  | position_ok? is true when a variable fits where it is used, a nullable variable with a default being allowed in a non-null place. |
| `toriyose::danpen::variable_problems(ex, op, where)` |  |  |  | variable_problems judges an operation's variables: their types and defaults, duplicates, uses with no definition, definitions with no use, and uses in the wrong position. |
| `toriyose::danpen::root_fields(ex, sels, seen)` |  |  |  | root_fields lists the response keys an operation selects at its root, through fragments, so a subscription can be held to one. |
| `toriyose::danpen::operation_problems(ex, op)` |  |  |  | operation_problems judges one operation: its root exists, a subscription selects one root field, and its variables and selections hold. |
| `toriyose::danpen::fragment_problems(ex, frag)` |  |  |  | fragment_problems judges one fragment definition: its type condition, a cycle through itself, and its selections. |
| `toriyose::danpen::executable_problems(s, doc)` |  |  |  | executable_problems judges an executable document against an indexed schema: document-wide rules first, then each operation and fragment. |
| `toriyose::danpen::as_document(x)` |  |  |  | as_document accepts text, a document or one definition, and answers a document. |
| `toriyose::danpen::schema_of(schema, opts?)` |  |  |  | schema_of indexes a schema once with its findings, so checking many documents against it does not index it again; an indexed schema passes through. |
| `toriyose::danpen::check(subject, rules?, opts?)` |  |  |  | check: masking, composition and entity-backed law findings for a program. |
| `toriyose::danpen::validate(document, schema?, opts?)` |  |  |  | validate: the document if every field and type is in the schema. |
| `toriyose::danpen::spec_selection(item)` |  |  |  | spec_selection turns one item of a fragment's `fields:` into selections: a name is a field, `"...Name"` a spread, `{name: [...]}` fields with sub-selections. |
| `toriyose::danpen::flat_selections(items)` |  |  |  | flat_selections turns a whole `fields:` list into one flat selection list. |
| `toriyose::danpen::fragment(name, spec)` |  |  |  | fragment: declare a named field selection on one schema type. |
| `toriyose::danpen::definitions_of_kind(doc, kind)` |  |  |  | definitions_of_kind: The definitions of a parsed document whose kind is `kind`, in document order. |
| `toriyose::danpen::fragments_of(x)` |  |  |  | fragments_of accepts a list of fragments as given, or takes the fragment definitions out of text or a document. |
| `toriyose::danpen::operation_of(frag)` |  |  |  | operation_of writes the operation a root-type fragment stands for, named in PascalCase after it and spreading it. |
| `toriyose::danpen::compose(first, second)` |  |  |  | compose: one operation from the union of the fragments a view uses. |
| `toriyose::danpen::response_key(sel)` |  |  |  | response_key is the key a field's value has in a response: its alias, else its name. |
| `toriyose::danpen::plain_hooks` |  |  |  | plain_hooks are the hooks `select` reads a plain value with: take each value as it is, skip an absent key, start from an empty map. |
| `toriyose::danpen::select_into(value, sels, hooks?)` |  |  |  | select_into reads only the selected keys of a value, through lists and nested selections, with hooks that let a caller resolve references or note absent keys. |
| `toriyose::danpen::select(value, fragment)` |  |  |  | select: only the fields a fragment declares, read from a value. |
| `toriyose::danpen::hash(operation, algorithm?)` |  |  |  | hash: the persisted-operation id of an operation's canonical text. |
| `toriyose::danpen::schema_table(sdl)` |  |  |  | schema_table reduces a valid schema to the field types of each composite type and the values of each enum, the data a client's masked read needs; an invalid schema raises. |
| `toriyose::danpen::table_entries(m)` |  |  |  | table_entries writes a map's entries as blue map lines, sorted by key, for the `:table` rendering. |
| `toriyose::danpen::table_text(document)` |  |  |  | table_text writes a schema's table as blue source, what `render(schema, :table)` answers. |
| `toriyose::danpen::fixtures(dir)` |  |  |  | fixtures reads the `.graphql` files under one fixture directory on the blue path as `[path, text]` pairs in path order; an invalid fixture's file name names the rule its text breaks. |
| `toriyose::danpen::rule_of(path)` |  |  |  | rule_of reads which rule an invalid fixture proves from its file name, the longest rule name winning so `fragment-spread-possible` is not read as a shorter rule. |
| `toriyose::danpen::rule_names` |  |  |  | rule_names lists every rule a finding can name, plus `parse`, so each has an invalid fixture to prove it fires. |
| `toriyose::danpen::rules_found(text, schema)` |  |  |  | rules_found names the rules a document breaks against a schema, a parse failure counting as the rule `parse`. |
| `toriyose::danpen::schema_named(name)` |  |  |  | schema_named indexes the schema fixture `fixtures/schema/NAME.graphql`, as `schema_of` indexes its text. |
| `toriyose::danpen::hand_schema` |  |  |  | hand_schema is the hand-written schema fixture the checker's tests judge documents against; it holds no findings. |
| `toriyose::danpen::documents_named(prefix, dirs)` |  |  |  | documents_named gathers the executable fixtures whose file name starts with `prefix` in each named directory, as `[path, text, schema]` rows checked against that directory's schema. |
| `toriyose::danpen::kinds_in(x)` |  |  |  | kinds_in names the kind of every record in a parsed tree, outermost first, so a test can prove the parser emits each kind the grammar lowers to. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::key_format` |  |  |  | key_format names the version of the key table, so a reader can refuse a table another layout wrote. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::separator` |  |  |  | separator joins the parts of one cache key, the colon hanabi's entity cache uses. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::root_key` |  |  |  | root_key stamps the root fields of a response, which belong to no entity. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::key(parts)` |  |  |  | key: one canonical cache key built from ordered parts. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::table?(x)` |  |  |  | table? tells a ready key table apart from a schema still to read. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::entity_type?(d)` |  |  |  | entity_type? tells whether a schema type is cached as its own entity: an object, not introspection, with an id. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::table_of(s)` |  |  |  | table_of builds the key table from a parsed schema: every entity type keyed by its id. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::schema(subject)` |  |  |  | schema: the entity key table a GraphQL schema implies. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::context(schema)` |  |  |  | context holds what a walk needs: the key table, and the parsed schema when one was given. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::ref?(x)` |  |  |  | ref? tells a reference to an entity apart from a value held in place. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::combined(x, y)` |  |  |  | combined lays a newer value over an older one, field by field, and keeps the fields only the older one has. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::entities_merged(a, b)` |  |  |  | entities_merged joins two entity tables so each key keeps one combined copy. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::field_type(ctx, parent, name)` |  |  |  | field_type finds the type a field of a parent type holds, so a walk can key a nested value that carries no typename. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::entity_key(ctx, t, rec)` |  |  |  | entity_key gives the cache key of a record of a cached type, or nil when the record lacks a key field. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::walk(value, type, ctx)` |  |  |  | walk splits one value into what stays in place, with references, and the entities it found. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::payload(response)` |  |  |  | payload takes the data of a GraphQL response, or the value itself when it holds no data field. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::normalize(response, schema, opts?)` |  |  |  | normalize: split a response into entities keyed by Type:id. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::merge(a, b)` |  |  |  | merge: combine two stores keeping one copy per entity. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::overlay(store)` |  |  |  | overlay lays every pending optimistic write over a store, so a read sees them. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::hooks(v)` |  |  |  | hooks tells danpen's select how to follow references through a store and to miss on an absent field. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::selections_of(f)` |  |  |  | selections_of takes the selections of a fragment or operation that a read answers. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::start_value(v, at)` |  |  |  | start_value picks where a read starts: the root, or the entity an `at:` names. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::null_at?(o)` |  |  |  | null_at? tells a read given `at: nil` apart from a read given no `at:`. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::fetched(v, o)` |  |  |  | fetched reads the fields of a fragment from a store, or nil when the store misses one. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::refs_in(x)` |  |  |  | refs_in lists every entity a read value references, so its age can be checked. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::rules_for(o)` |  |  |  | rules_for finds the policy rules of the fragment a read names. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::aged?(v, data, o)` |  |  |  | aged? tells whether any entity a read touched is older than its fragment's ttl. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::read_options` |  |  |  | read_options lists the options a read and a staleness check accept. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::read(store, opts)` |  |  |  | read: the masked fields of one fragment read from the store. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::stale?(store, opts)` |  |  |  | stale?: whether a fragment read is older than its ttl. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::rule_kinds` |  |  |  | rule_kinds holds the kousei schema of each fragment rule: ttl, stale, persist and evict. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::rule_value(e)` |  |  |  | rule_value takes the one setting of a kousei rule entry. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::policy(fragments, entries)` |  |  |  | policy: per fragment ttl, stale, persist and evict rules from kousei entries. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::types_with(p, kind)` |  |  |  | types_with gives each entity type the smallest value one of its fragments sets for a rule. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::kept(store, keeps)` |  |  |  | kept keeps only the entities a test admits, with their stamps. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::snapshot(store, opts?)` |  |  |  | snapshot: the entities persisted fragments read, to keep across restarts. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::sweep(store, opts)` |  |  |  | sweep: drop the entities older than their fragment evict limit. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::declare(name, spec)` |  |  |  | declare a mutation and the entity types it touches. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::touched(patch)` |  |  |  | touched lists the entity types a patch writes. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::write_machine` |  |  |  | write_machine is the lifecycle of one optimistic write: pending until confirmed or rolled back. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::blank` |  |  |  | blank is the empty store a first write starts from. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::write(store, change)` |  |  |  | write: lay a pending optimistic write over the store. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::outcome_event(outcome)` |  |  |  | outcome_event turns a settle outcome into the event the write machine takes. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::settle(store, id, outcome)` |  |  |  | settle: end an optimistic write as confirmed or rolled back. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::persisted_body(doc, variables)` |  |  |  | persisted_body builds the request body that names an operation by its hash and sends no query text. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::request(target, input, opts?)` |  |  | `B0084` | request: run an operation on a server by its persisted hash only. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::sdl` |  |  |  | sdl is the blog schema the tests and examples cache: posts, comments and users. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::user_card` |  |  |  | user_card is the fragment a user view reads: the user's id and name. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::comment_item` |  |  |  | comment_item is the fragment a comment view reads, with its author through user_card. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::post_page` |  |  |  | post_page is the fragment a post view reads, nesting comment_item and user_card. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::page_query` |  |  |  | page_query is the post page operation, composed with the fragments it spreads. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::operation_of(doc)` |  |  |  | operation_of takes the first operation of a document, the thing a root read answers. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::ada` |  |  |  | ada is one user the sample response holds twice, to show one shared entity. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::response` |  |  |  | response is the sample post page response the tests normalize. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::renamed(id, name)` |  |  |  | renamed is the normalized response of a rename mutation, used as an optimistic patch. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::entity_keys(s)` |  |  |  | entity_keys lists the keys of a store's entities in order, for comparing stores. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::unref(x)` |  |  |  | unref removes the reference marks a store read adds, so a read compares with a plain selection. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::users` |  |  |  | users is the pool drawn responses pick authors from. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::drawn_user(seed)` |  |  |  | drawn_user draws an author from users for a seed, or nil for a missing author. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::drawn_post(seed, pid)` |  |  |  | drawn_post draws a post with its comments for a seed and a post id. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::drawn_response(seed)` |  |  |  | drawn_response draws a posts response for a seed, for the normalize then read property. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::posts_query` |  |  |  | posts_query is the operation that selects every field a drawn response holds. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::server_text` |  |  |  | server_text is the program of the stand-in server: it answers one request with a canned reply and records what it was sent. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::waited(path, tries)` |  |  |  | waited waits for a file the stand-in server writes, and gives up with a timeout after a number of tries. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::hanabi_double(reply)` |  |  | `B0083` | hanabi_double starts the stand-in server for a reply in its own process, and gives the directory it writes to. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::user_view` |  |  |  | user_view is the component that shows one user through the user_card fragment. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::comment_view` |  |  |  | comment_view is the component that shows one comment through the comment_item fragment. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::post_view` |  |  |  | post_view is the component that shows one post through the post_page fragment. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::page_of(store)` |  |  |  | page_of builds the post page from a store, each view reading its own fragment. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::fake(status, reply)` |  |  |  | fake is a transport that answers with what it was sent and a reply, so a test sees the request body. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::echoed(status, reply)` |  |  |  | echoed is a transport that answers with a status and a reply only. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::page_html` |  |  |  | page_html is the HTML the post page must render to. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::ssr(url, opts)` |  |  |  | ssr renders the post page on a server from one request, and checks it. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::mailbox(acc)` |  |  |  | mailbox drains the messages this process holds, in order. |
| `toriyose::kyasshu::persisted?(body)` |  |  |  | persisted? tells whether a request body names the page query by hash and sends no query text. |
| `toriyose::outou::errors_of(result)` |  |  |  | errors_of: the GraphQL errors a refused parse, validation or request carries, ready to answer as `errors`. |
| `toriyose::outou::type_ref(text)` |  |  |  | type_ref: a type reference read from its GraphQL text, so a schema can name a type the way SDL writes it. |
| `toriyose::outou::named(name)` |  |  |  | named: a reference to the type called `name`, with no list or non-null wrapper. |
| `toriyose::outou::list_of(ref)` |  |  |  | list_of: a list of the type `ref`, given as a reference or as its text. |
| `toriyose::outou::non_null(ref)` |  |  |  | non_null: the type `ref` made non-null, so a value of it can never be null. |
| `toriyose::outou::as_ref(ref)` |  |  |  | as_ref: a type reference from either its text or a reference already built, so every constructor takes both. |
| `toriyose::outou::non_null?(ref)` |  |  |  | non_null? tells whether the reference `ref` forbids null. |
| `toriyose::outou::nullable(ref)` |  |  |  | nullable: the reference `ref` with its outer non-null wrapper taken off; a nullable reference stays as it is. |
| `toriyose::outou::deprecation(d)` |  |  |  | deprecation: the deprecation reason a `deprecated` option gives: true reads as the specification's default reason, a text stays itself, and nil means not deprecated. |
| `toriyose::outou::field(name, ref, opts?)` |  |  |  | field: one field of an object or interface type, with the arguments it takes, the resolver that answers it, the subscribe function of a subscription root and its deprecation. |
| `toriyose::outou::arg(name, ref, opts?)` |  |  |  | arg: one argument of a field or directive, or one field of an input type, with the default it falls back to when the request leaves it out. |
| `toriyose::outou::enum_value(name, opts?)` |  |  |  | enum_value: one value of an enum type, with its deprecation and description. |
| `toriyose::outou::as_enum_value(v)` |  |  |  | as_enum_value: an enum value from either its bare name or a value already built, so enum_type takes both. |
| `toriyose::outou::indexed(fields)` |  |  |  | indexed: a list of named members keyed by their names, so execution finds a field without a scan. |
| `toriyose::outou::object_type(name, fields, opts?)` |  |  |  | object_type: an object type of a schema, with its fields and the interfaces it implements. |
| `toriyose::outou::interface_type(name, fields, opts?)` |  |  |  | interface_type: an interface type of a schema, with its fields and the resolve_type function that names the object type of a value at runtime. |
| `toriyose::outou::union_type(name, members, opts?)` |  |  |  | union_type: a union type of a schema over the object types it may hold. |
| `toriyose::outou::enum_type(name, values, opts?)` |  |  |  | enum_type: an enum type of a schema; each value is a bare name or an enum_value. |
| `toriyose::outou::input_type(name, fields, opts?)` |  |  |  | input_type: an input object type of a schema, whose fields are built with arg. |
| `toriyose::outou::scalar_type(name, opts?)` |  |  |  | scalar_type: a custom scalar type of a schema, with the functions that serialize its values out and parse them in. |
| `toriyose::outou::directive(name, locations, args, opts?)` |  |  |  | directive: a directive a schema declares, with the locations it may appear at and whether it may repeat there. |
| `toriyose::outou::builtin_scalars` |  |  |  | builtin_scalars: the five scalar types every schema holds, as the specification describes them. |
| `toriyose::outou::builtin_directives` |  |  |  | builtin_directives: the four directives every schema declares, skip, include, deprecated and specifiedBy. |
| `toriyose::outou::output_kind?(k)` |  |  |  | output_kind? tells whether a type of kind `k` may be a field's type. |
| `toriyose::outou::input_kind?(k)` |  |  |  | input_kind? tells whether a type of kind `k` may be an argument's or a variable's type. |
| `toriyose::outou::leaf_kind?(k)` |  |  |  | leaf_kind? tells whether a value of a type of kind `k` is answered whole, with no selection set below it. |
| `toriyose::outou::composite_kind?(k)` |  |  |  | composite_kind? tells whether a type of kind `k` is answered through a selection set. |
| `toriyose::outou::kind_named(types, name)` |  |  |  | kind_named: the kind of the type called `name` in the type map `types`, or nil when no type has that name. |
| `toriyose::outou::ref_whys(types, owner, ref, ok_kind)` |  |  |  | ref_whys: why the reference `ref` that `owner` holds is wrong, an unknown type or a kind `ok_kind` refuses; empty when it is sound. |
| `toriyose::outou::type_whys(types, t)` |  |  |  | type_whys: every reason the type `t` cannot stand in a schema of `types`: a field or argument of an unknown or wrong kind, an implemented name that is no interface, a union member that is no object. |
| `toriyose::outou::root_or(spec_name, types, fallback)` |  |  |  | root_or: the root type of an operation: the name a spec gives, else `fallback` when `types` defines it, else nil. |
| `toriyose::outou::schema(spec)` |  |  |  | schema: a schema from a spec of types and root names, joined with the built-in scalars, directives and introspection types; a broken reference raises :invalid_schema with every reason at once. |
| `toriyose::outou::lookup(sc, name)` |  |  |  | lookup: the type called `name` in the schema `sc`, or nil when the schema has none. |
| `toriyose::outou::root_name(sc, operation)` |  |  |  | root_name: the root type that answers an operation of the kind `operation`, or nil when the schema has none. |
| `toriyose::outou::possible_types(sc, name)` |  |  |  | possible_types: the object types a value of the type `name` may be at runtime: itself for an object, its members for a union, its implementers for an interface. |
| `toriyose::outou::applies?(sc, object_name, condition)` |  |  |  | applies? tells whether a fragment on the type `condition` applies to an object of the type `object_name`. |
| `toriyose::outou::overlap?(sc, a, b)` |  |  |  | overlap? tells whether one runtime object type could be both of the types `a` and `b`. |
| `toriyose::outou::typename_field` |  |  |  | typename_field: the `__typename` meta-field every object answers with its own type name. |
| `toriyose::outou::field_def(sc, tname, fname)` |  |  |  | field_def: the field `fname` of the type `tname`, the meta-fields `__typename`, and `__schema` and `__type` on the query root, included; nil when the type has no such field. |
| `toriyose::outou::type_view(sc, ref)` |  |  |  | type_view: the introspection parent of a `__Type`: the reference `ref` seen in the schema `sc`. |
| `toriyose::outou::type_view_named(sc, name)` |  |  |  | type_view_named: the introspection view of the type called `name`, or nil when the schema has none, as `__type(name:)` answers. |
| `toriyose::outou::def_of(p)` |  |  |  | def_of: the type definition a named type view stands for; nil for a list or non-null view. |
| `toriyose::outou::kind_of_view(p)` |  |  |  | kind_of_view: the `__TypeKind` a type view answers: its definition's kind, or LIST or NON_NULL for a wrapper. |
| `toriyose::outou::kind_in?(p, kinds)` |  |  |  | kind_in? tells whether a type view names a definition of one of `kinds`, which decides the introspection fields it answers. |
| `toriyose::outou::shown_deprecated(xs, include)` |  |  |  | shown_deprecated: the members of `xs` introspection shows: all of them when `include` is true, else only those not deprecated. |
| `toriyose::outou::view_fields(p, include)` |  |  |  | view_fields: the `__Field` parents of an object or interface view, deprecated ones only when `include` is true; nil for any other kind. |
| `toriyose::outou::view_interfaces(p)` |  |  |  | view_interfaces: the views of the interfaces an object or interface view implements; nil for any other kind. |
| `toriyose::outou::view_possible(p)` |  |  |  | view_possible: the views of the object types an interface or union view may be at runtime; nil for any other kind. |
| `toriyose::outou::view_enum_values(p, include)` |  |  |  | view_enum_values: the `__EnumValue` parents of an enum view, deprecated ones only when `include` is true; nil for any other kind. |
| `toriyose::outou::view_input_fields(p)` |  |  |  | view_input_fields: the `__InputValue` parents of an input object view; nil for any other kind. |
| `toriyose::outou::view_of_type(p)` |  |  |  | view_of_type: the view of the type a list or non-null view wraps, as `ofType` answers; nil for a named view. |
| `toriyose::outou::view_description(p)` |  |  |  | view_description: the description of the type a view names; nil for a wrapper view. |
| `toriyose::outou::view_specified_by(p)` |  |  |  | view_specified_by: the URL that specifies a scalar view's behaviour, as `specifiedByURL` answers; nil for any other kind. |
| `toriyose::outou::view_name(p)` |  |  |  | view_name: the name a type view answers; nil for a list or non-null view, which has none. |
| `toriyose::outou::default_value_text(p)` |  |  |  | default_value_text: an argument's default written as GraphQL text, as `defaultValue` answers; nil when it has none. |
| `toriyose::outou::include_arg` |  |  |  | include_arg: the `includeDeprecated` argument the introspection fields and enumValues take, false unless given. |
| `toriyose::outou::introspection_types` |  |  |  | introspection_types: the types of the introspection system every schema holds, each resolved over the schema itself. |
| `toriyose::outou::entry_of(m, name)` |  |  |  | entry_of: whether the map `m` holds the key `name`, and its value, as a pair, so a present null is told apart from an absent key; a keyword key answers to its text. |
| `toriyose::outou::shown_value(v)` |  |  |  | shown_value: a value as an error message quotes it, as JSON when it has a JSON form. |
| `toriyose::outou::int_range?(v)` |  |  |  | int_range? tells whether a number fits GraphQL's Int, a signed 32-bit integer. |
| `toriyose::outou::enum_names(t)` |  |  |  | enum_names: the names of the values of the enum type `t`, which bound what it accepts and answers. |
| `toriyose::outou::coerce_scalar_input(t, v)` |  |  |  | coerce_scalar_input: a variable's value read as the scalar type `t`, by the specification's input coercion; a value it cannot represent raises :coerce. |
| `toriyose::outou::coerce_input(sc, ref, v)` |  |  |  | coerce_input: a variable's value read as the type `ref`: null refused where non-null, a single value lifted into a list, and each named type coerced by its kind. |
| `toriyose::outou::coerce_named_input(sc, t, v)` |  |  |  | coerce_named_input: a variable's value read as the named type `t`, a scalar, an enum or an input object. |
| `toriyose::outou::coerce_input_object(sc, t, v)` |  |  |  | coerce_input_object: a variable's map read as the input object type `t`: an unknown field refused, a missing one given its default, a missing required one refused. |
| `toriyose::outou::ast_value(ast)` |  |  |  | ast_value: the plain value a literal node of a document holds, a number read from its text. |
| `toriyose::outou::loc_of(node)` |  |  |  | loc_of: the `locations` of an error about a document node: its line and column, or none when the node has no span. |
| `toriyose::outou::literal_scalar(t, ast, vars)` |  |  |  | literal_scalar: a literal written in a document read as the scalar type `t`, by the specification's literal coercion; a literal it cannot represent raises :coerce. |
| `toriyose::outou::plain_value(ast, vars)` |  |  |  | plain_value: a value node of a document as a plain value, its variables read from `vars`, for a custom scalar to parse. |
| `toriyose::outou::value_from_ast(sc, ref, ast, vars)` |  |  |  | value_from_ast: a value node of a document read as the type `ref`, variables taken from the coerced `vars`; a value the type refuses raises :coerce. |
| `toriyose::outou::named_from_ast(sc, t, ast, vars)` |  |  |  | named_from_ast: a value node of a document read as the named type `t`, a scalar, an enum or an input object. |
| `toriyose::outou::object_from_ast(sc, t, ast, vars)` |  |  |  | object_from_ast: an object literal of a document read as the input object type `t`: an unknown field refused, a missing one given its default, a missing required one refused. |
| `toriyose::outou::render_value(sc, ref, v)` |  |  |  | render_value: a value of the type `ref` written as a GraphQL literal, as a default value is shown in introspection and SDL. |
| `toriyose::outou::coerce_arguments(sc, defs, nodes, vars)` |  |  |  | coerce_arguments: the argument values a field is resolved with, from the argument nodes written and the definitions `defs`: defaults filled, a missing required argument or an invalid value raised as :coerce. |
| `toriyose::outou::coerce_variables(sc, op, given)` |  |  |  | coerce_variables: the variables of the operation `op` coerced from the values `given`, with an error for each variable that cannot be, so a request with a bad variable answers every fault and no data. |
| `toriyose::outou::operations(doc)` |  |  |  | operations: the operations a document defines, in order. |
| `toriyose::outou::fragment_defs(doc)` |  |  |  | fragment_defs: the fragments a document defines, in order. |
| `toriyose::outou::fragments_of(doc)` |  |  |  | fragments_of: a document's fragments keyed by name, so a spread finds the fragment it names. |
| `toriyose::outou::request_error(message, locations)` |  |  |  | request_error: a request refused with one GraphQL error, before any field runs. |
| `toriyose::outou::operation_of(doc, name)` |  |  |  | operation_of: the operation of a document a request runs: the one called `name`, or the only one when no name is given; any other case is refused with the specification's error. |
| `toriyose::outou::response_key(node)` |  |  |  | response_key: the key a field node answers under in the response, its alias when it has one, else its name. |
| `toriyose::outou::directive_value(env, dirs, name)` |  |  |  | directive_value: the `if` argument of the directive `name` among `dirs`, read with the request's variables; nil when the directive is absent. |
| `toriyose::outou::included?(env, dirs)` |  |  |  | included? tells whether a selection carrying the directives `dirs` runs: not skipped, and not left out by include. |
| `toriyose::outou::add_to_group(groups, key, node)` |  |  |  | add_to_group: the ordered field groups with `node` added under the response key `key`, so fields of one key merge and keep their first position. |
| `toriyose::outou::collect_fields(env, tname, sels)` |  |  |  | collect_fields: the fields a selection set asks of an object of the type `tname`, grouped by response key in order, with fragments that apply expanded and skipped selections left out. |
| `toriyose::outou::collect_into(env, tname, sels, st)` |  |  |  | collect_into: the collection state `st`, field groups and fragments already visited, carried through every selection of `sels`. |
| `toriyose::outou::collect_one(env, tname, s, st)` |  |  |  | collect_one: the collection state `st` after the one selection `s`, a field, a fragment spread visited once, or an inline fragment that applies. |
| `toriyose::outou::done(v)` |  |  |  | done: the outcome of a field that completed with the value `v` and no error. |
| `toriyose::outou::failed(message, node, path)` |  |  |  | failed: the outcome of a field that raised a field error at `path`, located at its node, so null propagates from it. |
| `toriyose::outou::response(data, errs)` |  |  |  | response: an executed operation as the response shape of the specification: data, with errors only when there are some. |
| `toriyose::outou::exec_selection_set(env, tname, sels, parent, path)` |  |  |  | exec_selection_set: a selection set run against the value `parent` of the type `tname`: each field group resolved and completed into one map, its errors gathered. |
| `toriyose::outou::default_resolve(parent, name)` |  |  |  | default_resolve: the value of a field that has no resolver, read from its parent under the field's name. |
| `toriyose::outou::exec_field(env, tname, parent, nodes, path)` |  |  |  | exec_field: one field group of the type `tname` run: arguments coerced, the resolver called, the value completed; a raised error becomes a field error at `path`, and an unknown field answers nil. |
| `toriyose::outou::complete(env, ref, nodes, v, path, owner)` |  |  |  | complete: a resolved value completed as the type `ref`: a null in a non-null place is a field error that propagates, and a nullable place absorbs an error below it as null. |
| `toriyose::outou::complete_raw(env, ref, nodes, v, path, owner)` |  |  |  | complete_raw: a resolved value completed as the nullable type `ref`: a list item by item, a leaf serialized, an object or abstract type run through its selection set. |
| `toriyose::outou::complete_object(env, tname, nodes, v, path)` |  |  |  | complete_object: the value `v` completed as the object type `tname`, through the merged selection sets of its field nodes. |
| `toriyose::outou::resolve_type(env, t, v)` |  |  |  | resolve_type: the object type a value of the abstract type `t` is at runtime, from the type's resolve_type function or else the value's own `__typename`. |
| `toriyose::outou::serialize(t, v)` |  |  |  | serialize: a resolved value answered as the leaf type `t`, by the specification's result coercion; a value it cannot represent raises :coerce. |
| `toriyose::outou::serialize_other(t, v)` |  |  |  | serialize_other: a resolved value answered as an enum or a custom scalar: an enum name checked against its values, a scalar through its serialize function. |
| `toriyose::outou::env_for(sc, doc, values, opts)` |  |  |  | env_for: what every field of one execution reads: the schema, the document's fragments, the coerced variables and the resolvers' context. |
| `toriyose::outou::prepared(sc, doc, opts)` |  |  |  | prepared: the operation a request runs and its environment, or the refusal that stops it first: no such operation, a bad variable, or a root the schema lacks. |
| `toriyose::outou::execute(sc, doc, opts?)` |  |  |  | execute: a parsed document run against the schema `sc` to a response, with the operation name, variables, root value and context `opts` gives; it does not validate. |
| `toriyose::outou::event_response(env, root, op, event)` |  |  |  | event_response: the response a subscription sends for one source event, the event run as the root value of the operation's selection set. |
| `toriyose::outou::subscribe(sc, doc, opts?)` |  |  |  | subscribe: a subscription made ready: the source the root field's subscribe function names, and a function from each event to its response; a document that is no subscription, or a field with no subscribe function, is refused. |
| `toriyose::outou::gql_error(message, locations)` |  |  |  | gql_error: one GraphQL error of a request, its message and where in the document it points. |
| `toriyose::outou::parse(text)` |  |  |  | parse: a request's text read as a document with spans, or refused with the syntax error and its location. |
| `toriyose::outou::user_type?(t)` |  |  |  | user_type? tells whether the type `t` is one a service declared, not a built-in scalar or an introspection type, so SDL writes it out. |
| `toriyose::outou::deprecated_text(d)` |  |  |  | deprecated_text: the `@deprecated` directive SDL writes after a member with the reason `d`; empty when it is not deprecated. |
| `toriyose::outou::arg_text(sc, a)` |  |  |  | arg_text: one argument or input field written as SDL, with its default when it has one. |
| `toriyose::outou::args_text(sc, args)` |  |  |  | args_text: a field's argument list written as SDL; empty when it takes none. |
| `toriyose::outou::field_text(sc, f)` |  |  |  | field_text: one field written as an indented SDL line, with its arguments and deprecation. |
| `toriyose::outou::braced_lines(lines)` |  |  |  | braced_lines: the member lines of an SDL definition inside braces; empty when there are none, so an empty type writes no braces. |
| `toriyose::outou::implements_text(t)` |  |  |  | implements_text: the `implements` clause SDL writes for the interfaces of the type `t`; empty when it implements none. |
| `toriyose::outou::type_sdl(sc, t)` |  |  |  | type_sdl: one type of a schema written as its SDL definition. |
| `toriyose::outou::roots_sdl(sc)` |  |  |  | roots_sdl: the `schema` block of SDL naming each root type the schema has. |
| `toriyose::outou::sdl(sc)` |  |  |  | sdl: the schema `sc` written as SDL, the text danpen validates a document against. |
| `toriyose::outou::findings(doc, sc)` |  |  |  | findings: every validation error of the document `doc` against the schema `sc`, each with its locations and the rule it breaks; empty for a valid document. |
| `toriyose::outou::validate(doc, sc)` |  |  |  | validate: the document `doc` accepted when it is valid against the schema `sc`, else refused with all of its findings. |
| `toriyose::outou::request(sc, text, opts?)` |  |  |  | request: a GraphQL request answered end to end: the text parsed, validated against the schema and executed, a parse or validation failure answered as errors with no data. |
| `toriyose::outou::model_scalar(k)` |  |  |  | model_scalar: the GraphQL scalar a model field kind is served as; nil for a kind with none. |
| `toriyose::outou::lower_first(s)` |  |  |  | lower_first: a type name with its first letter lowered, the name of the query field that fetches one of it. |
| `toriyose::outou::model_field(e, f)` |  |  |  | model_field: a model field of the entity `e` as a GraphQL field and its refusals: kept out when secret or not public, refused alone when its kind has no scalar. |
| `toriyose::outou::model_relation(e, r, names)` |  |  |  | model_relation: a model relation of the entity `e` as a GraphQL field and its refusals, refused alone when it names an entity not in `names`. |
| `toriyose::outou::kept(pairs)` |  |  |  | kept: the fields of the field-and-refusals pairs that were not left out. |
| `toriyose::outou::plural_of(e)` |  |  |  | plural_of: the name of the query field that lists an entity: its declared plural, else its lowered name with an s. |
| `toriyose::outou::root_fields(e)` |  |  |  | root_fields: the two query fields an entity adds to the root, one by its key and one listing all of it. |
| `toriyose::outou::from_model(model)` |  |  |  | from_model: the object types and query root an entity model derives, with a DateTime scalar when a field is a time, and each field or relation that cannot be served refused alone. |
| `toriyose::outou::datetime_text(v)` |  |  |  | datetime_text: a DateTime as it is answered: text passes through, and a whole number is read as Unix milliseconds and written in RFC 3339. |
| `toriyose::outou::teeburu_scalars` |  |  |  | teeburu_scalars: the model kind each teeburu field type is served as; a type absent here has no GraphQL scalar, and from_model refuses its field by name. |
| `toriyose::outou::teeburu_field(ename, fname, rec, omit)` |  |  |  | teeburu_field: one teeburu field as a model field: its name in camelCase, its type through teeburu_scalars, optional unless declared `null: false`, and public unless `omit` names it as `entity.field`. |
| `toriyose::outou::teeburu_relations(ent)` |  |  |  | teeburu_relations: the relations a teeburu entity declares, each with its name in camelCase and the entity it names in PascalCase. |
| `toriyose::outou::teeburu_reverse(declared, target)` |  |  |  | teeburu_reverse: the reverse relations of the entity `target`: for each relation in `declared` that names `target`, a many relation to the owner, named with the owner's plural. |
| `toriyose::outou::distinct_members(ename, members)` |  |  |  | distinct_members: the members of the type `ename` with each name kept once. The first member with a name stays; each later one is left out and refused as a name clash. |
| `toriyose::outou::from_teeburu(model, opts?)` |  |  |  | from_teeburu: a teeburu model as the model from_model reads: each entity a type named in PascalCase, each field and relation in camelCase, each field typed through teeburu_scalars and nullable unless declared `null: false`, and every relation also answered from the entity it names as the list of its owners. `omit` lists `entity.field` names kept out of GraphQL; a name used twice on one type is refused alone. |
| `toriyose::outou::http_params(req)` |  |  |  | http_params: the query, variables and operation name a GraphQL-over-HTTP request carries, from a GET's query string or a POST's JSON body; a POST of any other content type, a body that is not a JSON object, no query, or variables that are not an object is refused by name. |
| `toriyose::outou::params_of(b, bad)` |  |  |  | params_of: the query, variables and operation name of a decoded request `b`, or the result of `bad` with the reason. A request with no query, variables that are not an object, or an operation name that is not a string is bad. |
| `toriyose::outou::http_error(status, code, message)` |  |  |  | http_error: a JSON response with the HTTP `status` and one GraphQL error that carries `message` and the extension `code`. |
| `toriyose::outou::query_only?(text, name)` |  |  |  | query_only?: whether the operation `name` in `text` is a query. Text that does not parse, or names no operation it holds, is true here, so the request reports its own error. |
| `toriyose::outou::http_answer(sc, req, opts?)` |  |  |  | http_answer: a GraphQL-over-HTTP request answered through request as application/json: 200 when the answer carries data, 400 when it carries only errors, 405 for a GET that is not a query, 415 for a POST that is not JSON. `context` is a function of the HTTP request giving the resolvers' context; with none, the context is the request. |
| `toriyose::outou::http_routes(path, sc, opts?)` |  |  |  | http_routes: the kyuuji routes that serve a schema at one path, GET for a query string and POST for a JSON body, each answered by http_answer. |
| `toriyose::outou::with_resolvers(types, table)` |  |  |  | with_resolvers: the types `types` with the resolvers of `table`, keyed `Type.field`, laid over their fields, so a derived schema answers. |
| `toriyose::outou::people` |  |  |  | people: the characters the test fixture serves, two humans and a droid. |
| `toriyose::outou::person(id)` |  |  |  | person: the fixture character with the id `id`, or nil. |
| `toriyose::outou::fixture` |  |  |  | fixture: the schema the tests and examples run against, a Star Wars graph with an interface, a union, enums, an input type, a mutation, a subscription and fields that fail on purpose. |
| `toriyose::outou::shop_model` |  |  |  | shop_model: a teeburu model for tests: a customer and an order that names it, with one field of a type that has no GraphQL scalar. |
| `toriyose::outou::field_texts(t)` |  |  |  | field_texts: each field of the type `t` as `name: Type` text, in order. |
| `toriyose::outou::http_get(qs)` |  |  |  | http_get: a GET request to `/graphql` with the query string `qs`. |
| `toriyose::outou::http_post(content_type, body)` |  |  |  | http_post: a POST request to `/graphql` with the content type `content_type` and the body `body`. |
| `toriyose::outou::answered(sc, req, opts?)` |  |  |  | answered: the answer of http_answer to `req` as its status, its content type and its body read as JSON. |

## ukai

`ukai` 0.1.0 · needs: deeta, fairu, kyohi, retsu, shisutemu, shuugou, tesuto

ukai (迂回) — a detour: keep an overlay VPN reaching its control plane and relays while a full-tunnel corporate VPN owns the default route.

| definition | other name | shares a builtin's name | waived | what it does |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ukai::axes` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::axis_names` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::variant_named(axis, text)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::state(axes_map, payload)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::unread_among(m, names)` |  |  |  | unread_among: the names in `names` whose value in `m` is `:unread`, in order: the axes a reading could not fill. |
| `ukai::unread_axes(s)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::noop(reason)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::refused(reason)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::blind(what)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::decide(s)` |  |  |  | decide: noop, bypass, refused or blind, over a total state. A route this host applied through a gateway that is no longer its own is retracted beside any answer but blind, so a move between networks never strands it. |
| `ukai::decide_kind(s)` |  |  |  | decide_kind: the answer of `decide` before any retract. It is blind when an axis is unread; otherwise the first axis that settles the case gives noop or refused, and a state that passes every check is a bypass with its route set. |
| `ukai::route_set(s)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::retract_set(s)` |  |  |  | retract_set: the routes to take back for `s`. It carries the backend, table and priority of `s` and its stale hosts, unique and sorted. |
| `ukai::sorted(xs)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::outcome_label(o)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::row_key(s)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::verdict(s)` |  |  |  | verdict: the outcome label of `decide` over `s` after `fill` adds a payload. It is the cell a matrix row holds for that state. |
| `ukai::matrix_rows(text)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::variants(axis)` |  |  |  | variants: the variants of `axis` in ukai's axis table, in table order. |
| `ukai::every_state` |  |  |  | every_state: every total state over ukai's axes, one for each row of the decision matrix. |
| `ukai::flat_map_states(acc, axis, vs)` |  |  |  | flat_map_states: each state in `acc` once for each variant in `vs`, with `axis` set to that variant. |
| `ukai::matrix_text(states)` |  |  |  | matrix_text: the decision matrix for `states` as text. A header row names the axes and the outcome; each state then gives one row with the label of its decision. |
| `ukai::matrix_findings(text, states)` |  |  |  | matrix_findings: the findings where the matrix `text` disagrees with `verdict` over `states`. An empty list means the matrix holds. |
| `ukai::duplicates(rows)` |  |  |  | duplicates: a finding for each row key that `rows` holds more than once. |
| `ukai::ip_rule_add_argv(ip, host, table, priority)` |  |  |  | ip_rule_add_argv: the `ip rule` argv that adds the rule sending `host` to `table` at `priority`. |
| `ukai::fixture(name)` |  |  |  | fixture: the text of ukai's fixture file `name`. |
| `ukai::matrix_row(line)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::fill(s)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::capture(argv)` |  |  | `B0083` |  |
| `ukai::bad(why)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::from_capture(c, parser)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::netstat_argv` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::ifconfig_argv` |  |  |  |  |

| `ukai::route_delete_argv(host)` |  |  |  | route_delete_argv: the darwin `route` argv that deletes the host route to `host`. |

| `ukai::derp_map_argv(tailscale)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::netcheck_argv(tailscale)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::status_argv(tailscale)` |  |  |  |  |
| `ukai::ip_route_argv(ip)` |  |  |  |  |

## umu

`umu` 0.1.0 · needs: kyohi

umu (有無) — presence or absence, as a declared value: blue's Option.

| definition | other name | shares a builtin's name | waived | what it does |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `umu::some(v)` |  | yes |  | some wraps a present value; nil is refused, since nil is the absence an option replaces. |
| `umu::option?(o)` |  |  |  | option? answers whether a value is an option at all, `some` or `none`. |
| `umu::some?(o)` |  | yes |  | some? answers whether an option holds a value. |
| `umu::none?(o)` |  |  |  | none? answers whether an option holds nothing. |
| `umu::fold(o, on_none, on_some)` |  |  |  | fold calls `on_none` for a `none` and `on_some` with the value for a `some`, and refuses anything that is not an option. |
| `umu::value_or(o, default)` |  |  |  | value_or reads the value of a `some`, else `default`. |
| `umu::expect(o, why)` |  |  |  | expect reads the value of a `some` and raises :not_found with `why` for a `none`. |
| `umu::map(o, f)` |  | yes |  | map applies `f` to the value of a `some`; `f` answering nil is refused, through `some`. |
| `umu::and_then(o, f)` |  |  |  | and_then hands the value of a `some` to `f`, which answers an option itself. |
| `umu::or_else(o, f)` |  |  |  | or_else keeps a `some` and asks `f` for an option in place of a `none`. |
| `umu::kept(o, keep)` |  |  |  | kept answers `o` when `keep` is true, else `none`. |
| `umu::filter(o, keep)` |  | yes |  | filter keeps a `some` whose value `keep` accepts, else `none`. |
| `umu::zip(a, b)` |  | yes |  | zip pairs the values of two options, `none` when either is `none`. |
| `umu::from_nil(v)` |  |  |  | from_nil reads older nil-answering code as an option: nil is `none`, anything else `some`. |
| `umu::to_nil(o)` |  |  |  | to_nil hands an option to code that still reads nil: the value, or nil for `none`. |
| `umu::ok_or(o, r)` |  |  |  | ok_or turns an option into a kyohi result, the refusal `r` standing in for `none`. |
| `umu::to_option(r)` |  |  |  | to_option turns a kyohi result into an option: the value of an ok, and `none` for a refusal or for an ok of nil. |

## utsushi

`utsushi` 0.1.0 · needs: gitto, kyohi, moji, retsu, shisutemu

utsushi (写し, a transcription) — a part of a repository's history, shown as a history of its own: a lens picks the part, and each commit is written again over the part's tree, with its author, committer and message unchanged, so the same lens over the same commit gives the same commit id on every machine (docs/UTSUSHI.md).

| definition | other name | shares a builtin's name | waived | what it does |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `utsushi::sub(path)` |  |  |  | sub: The lens that keeps only the tree at `path`, which becomes the root. |
| `utsushi::prefix(path)` |  |  |  | prefix: The lens that moves the whole tree under `path`. |
| `utsushi::chain(lenses)` |  |  |  | chain: The lenses applied in order, each to what the one before it kept. |
| `utsushi::compose(lenses)` |  | yes |  | compose: The trees of several lenses side by side. Their output roots must not overlap, so each file of the result has one source and the result can be written back; an overlap is refused, naming both roots. |
| `utsushi::shown_root(r)` |  |  |  | shown_root is an output root as a refusal shows it: the whole tree is `/`. |
| `utsushi::clean(path)` |  |  |  | clean is a lens path without empty segments, refusing one that names no directory or leaves the tree. |
| `utsushi::out_root(lens)` |  |  |  | out_root is where a lens puts what it keeps: a prefix's path, or the whole tree. |
| `utsushi::under?(a, b)` |  |  |  | under? tells whether path a is b or inside it. |
| `utsushi::overlap?(a, b)` |  |  |  | overlap? tells whether two output roots can hold the same file. |
| `utsushi::parse(spec)` |  |  |  | parse: A lens from Josh's filter text: `:/a/b` is `sub("a/b")`, `:prefix=x` is `prefix("x")`, and filters written one after another are a chain. Any other filter is refused by name. |
| `utsushi::parse_one(part)` |  |  |  | parse_one is the lens one Josh filter names, without its leading `:`. |
| `utsushi::git_stdin(repo, input, args)` |  |  | `B0083` | git_stdin runs git in `repo` with `input` on its standard input and gives its trimmed output. |
| `utsushi::entry_of(line)` |  |  |  | entry_of is one `git ls-tree` line as its mode, type, id and name. |
| `utsushi::entries(repo, tree)` |  |  | `B0084` | entries is every entry of one tree, read with `git ls-tree`. |
| `utsushi::entry_line(e)` |  |  |  | entry_line is an entry as the `git mktree` line that writes it. |
| `utsushi::mktree(repo, es)` |  |  |  | mktree writes a tree of these entries and gives its id. |
| `utsushi::subtree(repo, tree, path)` |  |  | `B0084` | subtree is the id of the directory at `path` in a tree, or nil when there is none there. |
| `utsushi::wrap(repo, tree, path)` |  |  |  | wrap is a new tree that holds `tree` at `path`. |
| `utsushi::merge_trees(repo, a, b)` |  |  |  | merge_trees is one tree holding the entries of both, refusing a name that both hold as a file. |
| `utsushi::keep(repo, lens, tree)` |  |  |  | keep: The tree a lens keeps of `tree`, or nil when it keeps nothing. |
| `utsushi::person(rest)` |  |  |  | person is an author or committer header's name, email and date. |
| `utsushi::header_value(lines, key)` |  |  |  | header_value is the value of a commit header line, or nil when the commit has none. |
| `utsushi::commit_parts(repo, c)` |  |  | `B0084` | commit_parts is a commit's tree, author, committer, signature flag and exact message. |
| `utsushi::commit_tree(repo, msg_file, tree, parents, parts)` |  |  | `B0083` | commit_tree writes a commit of `tree` on `parents` with the author, committer and message of `parts`, and gives its id. |
| `utsushi::check_policy(opts)` |  |  |  | check_policy refuses a projection that names no signature policy, or one that is not built. |
| `utsushi::step(repo, lens, msg_file, state, row)` |  |  |  | step projects one commit, given as its `rev-list --parents` row: written again, mapped to a parent when its tree did not change, or dropped when the lens keeps nothing of it. |
| `utsushi::project(repo, lens, source, target, opts)` |  |  | `B0084` | project: The history of `source` seen through `lens`, written to the ref `target`. Returns the new head (nil when the lens kept nothing), how many commits were read, how many were written, and how many signatures were dropped. `opts` names the signature policy, and only `{signatures: :drop}` is built: a rewritten commit's old signature cannot verify. |
| `utsushi::at_fixed_time(repo, args)` |  |  | `B0083` | at_fixed_time runs git in `repo` as the fixture's author and committer at 1700000000 +0000, so the commits it writes have the same ids on every machine. |
| `utsushi::fixed_commit(repo, files, message)` |  |  |  | fixed_commit writes `files` as [path, text] pairs and commits them at the fixed time. |
| `utsushi::josh_fixture(name)` |  |  | `B0084` | josh_fixture is a fresh repository holding the four fixed-time commits the Josh ids were recorded on. |
| `utsushi::log_of(repo, ref)` |  |  | `B0084` | log_of is the commit ids reachable from `ref`, newest first. |
| `utsushi::lens_refused?(f)` |  |  |  | lens_refused? tells whether calling f raises `:lens_refused`. |

## zenbu

`zenbu` 0.1.0 · needs: angou, baito, bunsho, burauza, chokkan, chozou, deeta, eigyou, fairu, fuuin, gamen, gitto, gurafu, hadome, happa, heni, hikisuu, hizuke, hon, ibuki, isshou, jisho, junjo, junni, kaaneru, kakehashi, kansuu, kazu, keiyu, khipu, kiwari, kodou, koubou, kousei, koutei, kueri, kurasuta, kyohi, maakuappu, mitoshi, moji, mokuroku, okite, ongaku, ran, retsu, ronri, sabi, saishikou, seikika, seimei, settei, shinchoku, shinsatsu, shinsuu, shisutemu, shomei, shuugou, shuusei, suugaku, tesuto, todokede, toride, toriyose, touroku, ugoki, ukai, umu, utsushi

zenbu (全部) — the whole distribution, as ONE dependency.

| definition | other name | shares a builtin's name | waived | what it does |
|---|---|---|---|---|
